  round trip: buy N lots in one market order, sell N 10 s later, net ticks/lot after fees (raw; paired = minus the twin's mid change)
    N  1: raw -1.351 +- 0.152, paired -1.251 +- 0.060 (90 round trips)
    N 10: raw -0.969 +- 0.160, paired -0.869 +- 0.076 (90 round trips)

  market orders fill at pre-impact prices: a round trip of N lots gains ~0.050 N ticks/lot from its own impact; ScalpEnv
